Title: Libro de las quatro postrimerias del hombre, en lengua tagala, y letra española
Creator: Francisco de San José, O.P., preacher general of the Province of the Most Holy Rosary
Publisher: Manila: en el Collegio y Universidad de Santo Thomas, por Gerónimo Correa de Castro, 1734 (the imprint line is partly garbled in the scan)
Publication Date: 1734
Rights: Public Domain (published 1734, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'The Newberry makes its collections available for any lawful purpose, commercial or non-commercial, without licensing or permission fees to the library, subject to these terms and conditions: https://www.newberry.org/policies#open-access')
Language: tgl
Source: Internet Archive item ayer_bt_820_f73_1734 (https://archive.org/details/ayer_bt_820_f73_1734), the item's own ayer_bt_820_f73_1734_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: not named in the item metadata; host metadata date: 1734. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: a91a282af2626709260f41c55786309563733475269c072dc3a84eafa02d6338.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Newberry Library (Ayer collection) scan; usable OCR of the Tagalog with the long s read as f, Spanish chapter heads and scripture references in the margins.
Content note: A Tagalog devotional book on the four last things (death, judgement, hell, heaven) by a Dominican of the first generation (the host dates him 1560-1614), reprinted at the Dominican press in Manila in 1734: meditations with scripture and examples for Tagalog readers.
